> **DEFECT BASE (V13X):** every cut, rank, lead and withdrawal on this page was computed on D7 V13X, which dropped the TH-L2 loss cut and take-line exits that came before the resting add filled (846 rows; BASE-4 fixed it in V13Y). Treat all numbers here as 'defect base (V13X)'; the V13Y re-run of the leads is in progress and will supersede them.

# DS3_ZONES_3_L2_TAPE_NEWCAP - tape zone streams on the NEW cap (to 2026-10-30), scored in the ZONES frames (ZL-2, s7)

**Base: D7 v1.3 EXACT (V13X, BASE-4: exact U7, exact NY-AM lots, the five exact profit lines, per-book 12R lock) - HEADLINE.** Research only; post-hoc, Shadow only, no PASS claims. New windows only (L1M L2M VIEW, L3M L6M HOLD MID PRE X'; Feb-Oct = F9 = 2026-02-07..10-31). Old-cap results are on `DS3_ZONES_3_L2_TAPE.md` (OLD CAP). Prereg autopilot/ZL2_TAPE_prereg.md (streams) and ZL2_TAPE_SCORE_prereg.md (scoring; sha256 in DECISIONS.md). Code l2vast/ds3/code/zl2/, cells /mnt/l2/l2vast/ds3_zones3_new/ZL-2/ (cells/, cells_summary.csv, robust_f1.csv, plc_rank.csv, interim_tables.md), streams out/zl/streams_tape_new/.

## PAGE 1
**DATA.** NQ 1-s print tape from the DE-DUPLICATED months/ext weeks (`NQ_dropin_redo`) 2023-02..2026-10-30, 169,627,126 fp rows, path ds3_g2_redo; 16 roll days (13 old + 3 new: 2026-03-17, 06-16, 09-15) excluded from every stream by a tape/path coverage test. 126 streams (profile lines incl. the three missing ones, composites 5/20/60 d, developing nodes and voids, delta-at-price, cum-delta, large prints, measured pools). The pool level sources (swing/equal/session/killzone/prior-day H/L) were re-implemented on the extended path because the POI store has none of them after the old cap. Disclosure: the old-cap fp build already contained 2026-02 to 02-27 (the path array was longer than the cap); nothing scored used it.

**The base in these windows** (D7 v1.3 EXACT (V13X); lock 12R, live lots):

|---|---|---|---|---|

### Verdict in plain words (V13X = the EXACT D7 v1.3; the assumed-base page is DS3_ZONES_3_L2_TAPE_NEWCAP_ASSUMED.md)
* On the EXACT base the Feb-Oct maxDD is 78.5k (HOLD 119.2k, PRE 252.4k), carried by the week of 2026-03-30 - NOT the 2026-09-14 episode of the assumed base: the exact take lines move the drawdown into March. The assumed-base finding "one episode carries every cut" does not carry over; the exact base has its own episodes (MID 2026-03-23, PRE 2025-04-14, X' 2024-01-29 key weeks).
* **Gate counts over the 593 non-placebo V13X cells (404-cell individual-stream screen + 22 groups + variants):** STRICT 72, TOLERANT 268, NOW-ONLY 416. The STRICT and TOLERANT cells with a real cut are small-volume: **developing POC lines (TVP|poc day/sess) D40 skip** (1.8-3.5k rows = 2-4% of rows), **large prints >= 25 lots in the last 4 h (TLP p25 4h) D40 skip** (5.1k rows), session HVN2 (TPF hvn2 sess D40 skip). Their placebo ranks (100 draws per kind running) and the one-week / month checks are in the tables below; where a rank is missing the draws are still running.
* Volume-heavy cells (developing LVN/HVN zones, TPF session/day lines, cum-delta extremes) still show large cuts that random same-volume skips/halves reproduce: volume effects.
* Measured stop pools: unchanged (the pool known at formation does not predict the move beyond; swing / equal / session / killzone / prior-day H/L are run-through levels).
* Asia is the product in 2026: the session table (B7) lists which lines help Asia; the per-session columns of the finalists are below and in `b7_cells13x.csv` (every cell).

## Causality audit of the EXTENDED streams (2026) and the old-cap leak check
4 as-of cuts: **cut 0 = the OLD cap (end of label day 2026-02-06, epoch 1770415200)** and 3 random cuts in 2026-03..2026-10 (epochs 1778271469, 1776047091, 1778630492). Every generator (profiles, composites, developing nodes, delta-at-price, cum-delta, large prints, pools) was re-run on the EXTENDED tape truncated at the cut. For cut 0 ALL rows formed before the cap (856429 rows) were compared with the full build; for cuts 1-3 all rows formed in the 3 days before the cut. **865258 rows compared: 0 missing, 0 extra, max |d level| = 0.0, max |d strength| = 0.0.** Consequence: the first fp build, which already contained 2026-02 to 02-27, cannot have leaked into the old-cap scoring: every row formed before the cap is identical when the tape after the cap is removed, and the scoring only uses rows with ek <= the signal index.

## Streams on the new cap (descriptive, new windows)
Stream table and hit-rate table: `out/zl/desc_new/stream_table.csv` (all 126 rows; ranks vs random-level and shuffled-day placebos, 200 draws each; windows ALL X' PRE HOLD MID L6M L3M L1M).
* HOLD5 rank >= 0.975 against BOTH placebos, whole sample: TCP_hvn1_60d, TCP_vah_5d, TLP_cl25_4h, TPF_hvn2_sess (chance about 3 of 117). HOLD (2025-11..2026-10): TCP_hvn1_60d, TCP_lvn1_5d, TLP_p50_1h, TLP_p50_4h, TPF_hvn1_week, TPF_hvn2_sess, TPF_lvn0_sess. L6M: TCP_hvn1_60d, TPF_lvn0_sess, TPF_vah_sw15, TPF_val_sess, TVP_lvn_day.
* Run-through levels: 27 streams rank <= 0.025 against both placebos in the whole sample, 22 of them pool sources (swing / equal / session / killzone / prior-day H/L).

## Live definitions
Unchanged from the OLD CAP page, section 4 (`DS3_ZONES_3_L2_TAPE.md`): D7 computes TPF session/day/sw lines, TVP, TDA, TCD, TLP from its own print tape; composites, the burst threshold and the per-type median pool need the daily zones file.
